-- | Start position (line, column) of something.

type BNFC'Position = C.Maybe (C.Int, C.Int)

pattern BNFC'NoPosition :: BNFC'Position
pattern $mBNFC'NoPosition :: forall {r}. BNFC'Position -> ((# #) -> r) -> ((# #) -> r) -> r
$bBNFC'NoPosition :: BNFC'Position
BNFC'NoPosition = C.Nothing

pattern BNFC'Position :: C.Int -> C.Int -> BNFC'Position
pattern $mBNFC'Position :: forall {r}. BNFC'Position -> (Int -> Int -> r) -> ((# #) -> r) -> r
$bBNFC'Position :: Int -> Int -> BNFC'Position
BNFC'Position line col = C.Just (line, col)

-- | Get the start position of something.

class HasPosition a where
  hasPosition :: a -> BNFC'Position

instance HasPosition Program where
  hasPosition :: Program -> BNFC'Position
hasPosition = \case
    AProgram BNFC'Position
p [Unit' BNFC'Position]
_ -> BNFC'Position
p

instance HasPosition Unit where
  hasPosition :: Unit' BNFC'Position -> BNFC'Position

    UnitModule BNFC'Position
p Module' BNFC'Position
_ -> BNFC'Position
p
    UnitTelescope BNFC'Position
p TelescopeDecl' BNFC'Position
_ -> BNFC'Position
p

    AModule BNFC'Position
p VarIdent
_ [Include' BNFC'Position]
_ [Param' BNFC'Position]
_ [Import' BNFC'Position]
_ [Decl' BNFC'Position]
_ -> BNFC'Position
p

    AnInclude BNFC'Position

    NoRefinement BNFC'Position

    ARefinement BNFC'Position

    AFixed BNFC'Position

    DischargeOver BNFC'Position
